# Test source

```ts
  33  | 
  34  | export interface ProjectListEntry {
  35  |   name: string
  36  |   slug: string
  37  | }
  38  | 
  39  | export interface ProjectDescriptionRow {
  40  |   slug: string
  41  |   description: string | null
  42  | }
  43  | 
  44  | // ============================================
  45  | // Projects Page Component
  46  | // ============================================
  47  | 
  48  | export class ProjectsPage extends UiBase {
  49  |   constructor(options: TestContextOptions) {
  50  |     super(options);
  51  |   }
  52  | 
  53  |   // ============================================
  54  |   // Navigation (Public)
  55  |   // ============================================
  56  | 
  57  |   /**
  58  |    * Navigate to the Projects index. Call before any index-reading ATC.
  59  |    */
  60  |   @step
  61  |   async open(): Promise<void> {
  62  |     await this.page.goto(this.buildUrl('/projects'));
  63  |   }
  64  | 
  65  |   // ============================================
  66  |   // Helpers (Private)
  67  |   // ============================================
  68  | 
  69  |   private listItem(slug: string) {
  70  |     return this.page.locator(`[data-testid="projects-list-item-${slug}"]`);
  71  |   }
  72  | 
  73  |   // ============================================
  74  |   // ATCs - Complete Test Cases
  75  |   // ============================================
  76  | 
  77  |   /**
  78  |    * ATC: A workspace with projects lists them all, oldest first, with name
  79  |    * and slug — and the create form is not what greets the member.
  80  |    *
  81  |    * Staging is a SHARED workspace that already carries projects from other
  82  |    * test runs, so this asserts the seeded projects' relative order and
  83  |    * content among however many entries exist — not the list's total count
  84  |    * (that would make the ATC flaky against a workspace this session doesn't
  85  |    * own exclusively).
  86  |    *
  87  |    * @param expected - the seeded projects, already in oldest-first order
  88  |    */
  89  |   @atc('BK-604')
  90  |   async listProjectsOldestFirstWithNameAndSlug(expected: ProjectListEntry[]): Promise<void> {
  91  |     const list = this.page.locator('[data-testid="projects-list"]');
  92  |     await expect(list).toBeVisible();
  93  | 
  94  |     const items = list.locator('li');
  95  |     const testIds = await items.evaluateAll(nodes =>
```
